## INTRODUCTION

Blockchain is essentially a distributed, decentralized electronic record that keeps track of activities safely and irreversibly. Blockchain innovation \[[1](#9789815305210-C4.html#r1)\] operates on a network of peers, guaranteeing that no individual has total control over the data. This reduces the chance of information being altered or illicit access while boosting transparency. This feature is especially helpful in the field of healthcare, where patient confidentiality and security of information are crucial. The permanent nature of blockchain files adds a critical degree of safety for health information. Since every item or transaction on the blockchain is cryptographically linked to every other deal, amending previous data would need modifying each transaction that comes afterward. This unchangeable feature safeguards sensitive knowledge and health data, significantly reducing the likelihood of forgery and increasing the trust of patients. A full file of transactions is accessible to all participants in the network, facilitating regulatory adherence, product confirmation, and health information monitoring. The integration of computer science into healthcare \[[2](#9789815305210-C4.html#r2), [3](#9789815305210-C4.html#r3)\] gave rise to healthcare technology, which greatly enhanced healthcare. Blockchain computing has several uses in the medical field outside of patient information. These include medication safety, managing supply chains, sickness forecasting, medication accountability, claims processing, and others. The easy-to-use system and reliable environment of blockchain technology yield useful outcomes that increase parties' faith. The Hyperledger Fabric system (individual Blockchain) safeguards patient confidentiality and the integrity of their health information.

## An Overview of Blockchain Technology and its Version

Blockchain is a decentralized technology that allows several parties to conduct operations and keep track of them in a record. It is based on a dispersed network of nodes that securely and irrevocably log and confirm events. Every node in the network confirms every payment as it gets recorded to the blockchain, and its record is copied among all of the nodes \[[3](#9789815305210-C4.html#r3)^(\-)[5](#9789815305210-C4.html#r5)\]. Blockchain has two types: one is permission, and another is permissionless. The example is listed as follows:

• **Permissioned Blockchain**: Hyperledger Fabric.

• **Permissionless Blockchain**: Ethereum.

Since its establishment, from the initial digital currency to the present blockchain-based deployment for industry, blockchain technology has been utilized by numerous sectors as a component of the infrastructure of various enterprises that demand openness, honesty, and accuracy in Industry 5.0. Blockchain technology has advanced ([Table **1**](#9789815305210-C4.html#t1)) from version 1.0 to version 5.0, making it more trustworthy and appropriate for use in commercial settings and industrial uses.

Table 1 **Blockchain Technology Advancements.**  

| **Version** || **Description** |
| --- | --- |
| Blockchain 1.0 || The innovative digital currency system was driven by the configurable currency known as the digital currency bitcoin. The virtual currency's transfer architecture is decentralized and key-based, which is how the blockchain system got its beginnings. |
| --- | --- |
| Blockchain 2.0 || Blockchain-based programs have become prevalent in social domains like banking, transactions among peers, data legitimate enrollment, property and trademark approval, and smart leadership. These applications are based on the expandable culture. |
| Blockchain 3.0 || It expands the use of blockchain technology for decentralized apps (DApps), enhancing productivity and trust among people through decentralization, non-tampering, and reliable collaboration. |
| Blockchain 4.0 || An advancement over the prior version that uses the consensus protocol to regulate network behavior and increase the viability of DApps for real-time business situations applicable in the industrial age (IA) 4.0. |
| Blockchain 5.0 || The current version of the blockchain is regarded as an emerging generation due to eliminating conventional blockchain constraints and using simulated links for quicker execution and more protection. |

  

Blockchain versions 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, and 5.0 are in distinct deployment phases. They are all concurrent perspectives of growth, ranging from 1.0 to 5.0, and they have proper roles in various industries. Fig. ([**1**](#9789815305210-C4.html#f1)) shows the extent of blockchain innovation advancement.

![](Images/9789815305210-C4-F1.jpg)**Fig. (1))**  
The range of blockchain technology advancements. 

### Blockchain's Characteristics

• ***Peer-to-peer Connection Unchanging Distributed Record:*** Make sure that the blockchain's data structure maintains the structural permanence of the single node record.

• Privacy methods, like encryption, hash computations, and digital signatures, ensure the confidentiality and safety of operations.

• ***Agreement Techniques***: Utilizing technology to guarantee that consensus is reached, this pure logical mechanism for blockchain communal authentication builds confidence amongst all participants.

• ***Intelligent Agreement:*** It is a guaranteed reliable enterprise that conducts operations with no intervention from outside parties. Intelligent agreements are mainly employed to lower transaction expenses and offer safeguards for other contracts. As a result, intelligent contracts guarantee the legitimacy and dependability of each exchange that occurs between nodes.

• ***Being Transparent and Exchange***: Everyone with an equal right to access and use blockchain data can use its features.

• ***The Accord:***Certain nodes agree to finish checking and validate operations in just a portion of the time. The network's consensus is taken seriously if multiple nodes can agree without having to share assets in the deal.

• ***Competitive Rivalry:*** Algorithms compute the tasks of every node and establish accounting entitlements.

• ***Integrity and Reliability:*** Each document is meticulously and accurately produced while being closely monitored.

• ***Reliable and Dependable:*** Intricate checksum sharing ensures honesty, accessibility, and privacy; data encryption and cryptography techniques guard against data manipulation and forgery. An encryption method (digital signature) that requires every link to have its key and only transmits packets when the key is in an appropriate state allows for the detection of numerous hackers.

## HYPERLEDGER FRAMEWORK

### Hyperledger

The Ubuntu Foundation launched the Hyperledger initiative in the last month of 2015 as a collection of open-source blockchains and associated tools to facilitate the cooperative creation of distributed records based on blockchain technology \[[6](#9789815305210-C4.html#r6), [7](#9789815305210-C4.html#r7)\].

Hyperledger's “cover approach” fosters and advances a variety of enterprise blockchain structures, uses, archives, interactions, and technology. At present, Hyperledger is the home of the subsequent foundation developments.

• Hyperledger Fabric

• Hyperledger Sawtooth

• Hyperledger Iroha

• Hyperledger Indy

• Hyperledger Burrow

Among the above-mentioned hyperledger frameworks, we will discuss only one framework (*i.e.*, Hyperledger Fabric).

### HyperLedger Fabric (HLF)

One of the Hyperledger initiatives run by the Ubuntu Foundation is the blockchain architecture deployment known as Hyperledger Fabric. Fabric stands out as a permissioned network system where users have verified credentials. Its flexible design divides the handling of transactions into three phases, namely ordering, transaction validation and commitment, and decentralized reasoning execution and consensus (also known as “chain code”).

Using widely utilized and tested technology, Hyperledger Fabric is a distributed database for intelligent contracts with an extensible structure that enables adaptable solutions for various services \[[8](#9789815305210-C4.html#r8)^(\-)[10](#9789815305210-C4.html#r10)\]. Using a Byzantine Fault Tolerance agreement system, confirming peers run a duplicated state system that takes three different kinds of events as actions. Because the fabric uses a permissioned record, it has an encryption structure that allows permission and identification. Public key credentials enable registration and trade permission, while in-band cryptography guarantees chain code integrity. It adopts a unique reaching-an-agreement strategy that preserves confidentiality while enabling growth.

Before getting able to see the general design, one must comprehend the typical Hyperledger Fabric network pieces, as well as the ones that are exclusive to the suggested solution.

 

*   **Peer:** Associated with the points that comprise the system's structures. They are in charge of dispersing and keeping all of the data. These are the parts that provide the system's orderer nodes with knowledge so they can set up the processed units.
*   **Orderer:** One of the most crucial components of the network is the orderer. It is overseen by peers. They must come to the necessary agreement whether they are a part of one or more groups. They are involved in each exchange about the network setting. Additionally, systems use simple security measures for channels, limiting who can configure them as well as access and modify information to channels.
*   **Channel:** A channel is a way for people on the network to interact with one another. In this instance, it is an encrypted communication method that enables secrecy and information separation. This level is in charge of maintaining data protection and data transfer among network clients.
*   **Consensus:** The distributed ledger's present way of verifying blocks. There have been three distinct algorithms for the Fabric architecture over the years. At first, a method called Solo was created, enabling the presence of just one orderer network responsible for processing the blocks it creates. Simultaneously, a different algorithm, known as the Kafka algorithm, was developed. This algorithm is Byzantine fault-tolerant and only needs one node to handle every transaction. Over time, this approach made setup and upkeep more difficult. In light of these two, Raft's agreement emerges.
*   **Certification Authorities (CA):** They are equivalent to a common component of public key infrastructures(PKIs), which are in charge of distributing electronic certificates. The purpose of this tier is to verify that those involved or parties in the interaction are who they claim to be. To confirm that an internet site is trustworthy, credible CAs typically give electronic certificates on their behalf.
*   **Membership Service Provider (MSP):** It gathers all of the encryption methods applied to online communication. Every company needs an MSP, which houses its cryptographic data, including its secret keys and the certificate authority (CA) that granted it. Peers use this information to verify the outcomes of transaction processing (recommendations), while users use it to verify their activities.
*   **Chain Code:** In essence, chain code, also known as an intelligent contract, is the company's code that controls how different people or businesses in a blockchain network communicate or conduct business with one another. Chaincode calls produce collections and get the record or global state. It is an element of a program that is installed on other nodes in the context of Hyperledger Fabric connections to allow communication with the common ledger of those networks. Chaincode is separate from the validating peer process and operates in a safe Docker environment. Chaincode uses events that are uploaded by services to set up and preserve the state of the blockchain.

• Put into practice in the Go/Java/JavaScript language.

• Combined With Connections in a Module.

• The evaluating peer operates in a container using Docker.

### Orderer and Pluggable Ordering Service

In Hyperledger Fabric, an orderer's job is to group events in a block and organize them sequentially. Orderers are also in charge of allowing the establishment of networks \[[11](#9789815305210-C4.html#r11)^(\-)[14](#9789815305210-C4.html#r14)\]. Three different ordering setup types are available in Hyperledger Fabric.

 

*   Solo.
*   Kafka.
*   Raft.

#### Solo

As the name implies, the solo ordering system comprises just one node that handles everything (Fig. [**2**](#9789815305210-C4.html#f2)). It is not fault-tolerant hence it is not advised for true use in production. It is advised for proof of idea development or debugging.

![](Images/9789815305210-C4-F2.jpg)**Fig. (2))**  
Solo orderer for instance. 

#### Raft

A leader and the other nodes make up the raft ordering system. This network's controller is chosen by choice, following a reaching an agreement process. Supporters imitate the leader's behavior. Compared to Kafka, this ordering mechanism is significantly simpler to set up (Fig. [**3**](#9789815305210-C4.html#f3)).

![](Images/9789815305210-C4-F3.jpg)**Fig. (3))**  
Raft ordering assistance for instance. 

#### Kafka

An additional “the ruler and supporters” based system for ordering is built on Apache Kafka. Zookeepers are also needed to oversee the ordering networks. It has been accessible since Hyperledger Fabric v1.0. Still, it was difficult to adopt once Raft was released due to its greater administration costs and the need for complicated settings to be used with Hyperledger Fabric (Fig. **4**).

![](Images/9789815305210-C4-F4.jpg)**Fig. (4))**  
Kafka ordering assistance for instance. 

### State Repositories

CouchDB and LevelDB may both be utilized as state archives thanks to Hyperledger Fabric. The two databases listed above are open source, NoSQL-based, and cannot handle SQL queries or the relational database design. These two facilitate the fundamental functions of chain code, including retrieving a key-value conjunction, storing it, and doing queries using the keys. CouchDB, nevertheless, is recommended since it can execute sophisticated, complex searches. NoSQL databases provide an enormous number of options for managing information.

[Table **2**](#9789815305210-C4.html#t2) describes the difference between SQL and NoSQL databases.

Table 2 **Comparison Between SQL and NoSQL Database.**  

| **SQL Database** || **NoSQL Database** |
| --- | --- |
| Relational Database Management System(RDBMS) || Distributed Database |
| --- | --- |
| Schema Oriented || Schema Less |
| ACID(Atomicity,Consistency,Isolation,Durability)Property || BASE (Basically, Available, Soft State, Eventual Consistency) Property |
| Vertical Scaling || Horizontal Scaling |
| API Not Supported || API Supported |

  

In SQL database, there are complex queries that can becompared to those in the NoSQL database. Moreover, NoSQL is an open source, and it provides many applications, So, all medical-oriented data are stored in NoSQL databases. The types of NoSQL databases are as follows:

• Key/Value Database.

• Colum-oriented Database.

• Document-oriented Database.

• Graph-riented Database.

• Wide Row Database.

The concept of organizations with distinct rights and privacy practices for each organization is offered by Hyperledger. Additionally, company solutions call for the development of complicated data structures and industrial logic, which Hyperledger Fabric enables through the use of smart contracts \[[15](#9789815305210-C4.html#r15)^(\-)[17](#9789815305210-C4.html#r17)\], occasionally referred to as chain code.

The various kinds and operational details of each chain code are listed below:

• **Lifecycle System Chaincode (LSCC):** It is utilized in a hyper ledger fabric network for chain code setup, implementation, and refreshes.

• **Configuration System Chaincode (CSCC):** It is employed to manage peer-level network environments.

• **Query System Chaincode (QSCC):** It is employed to manage query registry APIs, such as retrieving transactions and blocks.

### CouchDB is Used to Connect Two Organizations

Two organizations, org1 and org2, with a pair of peers each, make up this network. While peer1 in both organizations is limited to committers, peer0 in both has installed chain code and is a supporter. Every organization has the right to certify people. CouchDB is the database of preference. The efficiency of a lone orderer will also be simulated using this network (Fig. **6**).

## BENEFITS OF BLOCKCHAIN IN HEALTHCARE

Blockchain is regarded as a versatile platform that applies to several industries, the most notable of which is healthcare. Because of the broad range of issues that can be resolved by the features and attributes provided by blockchain, the medical sector is vital and presents several prospects for using this concept. Decentralization is a key component of blockchain technology that is advantageous for applications in the healthcare sector \[[18](#9789815305210-C4.html#r18)^(\-)[21](#9789815305210-C4.html#r21)\]. It enables dispersed application deployment without depending on a single central entity. Patients and other healthcare users can be informed about how, by whom, and when their information is being utilized because of the openness and honesty created by the blockchain's data replication across every network node. Because of the way it works, it can shield information from grief, fraud, or safety hazards. Additionally, because of its inviolability, nothing that has been linked to the chain can be changed or altered, guaranteeing the reliability and accuracy of patient files. It is ensured that only individuals with authorized access privileges can decode the data by using encryption methods that secure it, enhancing its confidentiality and safety. Additionally, because digital keys are being used and the patients on the blockchain have false identities, data about them can be exchanged across partners without revealing their recognition.

![](Images/9789815305210-C4-F6.jpg)**Fig. (6))**  
CouchDB is used to connect two organizations. 

### Leveraging Hyperledger Fabric for Digital Wellness Data (DWD)

Digital wellness data (DWD) is a type of medical records system that is utilized to handle, exchange, and store clinical information concerning patients. These networks have grown significantly in the past few decades and have long been a significant aspect of the healthcare sector \[[22](#9789815305210-C4.html#r22)\]. This is mostly because of the computerization of medical structures, which has made it more necessary than ever to gather and distribute enormous quantities of information. DWD has been employed to raise the standard of healthcare for patients by giving medical professionals timely knowledge of critical health data. It provides various benefits to the field of health research besides helping handle data for medical examination and therapy.

Blockchain technology provides numerous benefits for safeguarding the confidentiality of patients in DWD.

• **Better Safety for Information (BSI):** By keeping healthcare data in an interconnected system that is impervious to outside abuse, the blockchain provides greater safety.

• **Greater Openness (GO):** People can keep an eye on how their information is being utilized thanks to the blockchain, which assists in avoiding data abuse.

• **More Seclusion (MS):** Individuals can manage who has possession of their information thanks to the blockchain. This may lessen the likelihood of hacking and abuse.

When it comes to highly confidential information like electronic health records, blockchain offers perks. Blockchain technology protects and monitors transmissions of information through encryption, making it hard to change or falsify information without being caught. Because blockchain is distributed, there is not one single source of collapse, which lowers the possibility of hacks and information thefts.

### Powered by Blockchain Healthcare Deployment

Healthcare and wellness information includes clinical information gathered during medical treatment along with private prevention of illness. The platform is a collaboration platform that combines technological advances with health information. Three elements make up the framework: data assistance, the privacy of data, and data acquisition. The safeguarding component creates a safeguard for health assistance, the data gathering module gathers health data about patients, and the service function fulfills patient demands for health records.

### A Blockchain System for Preserving Medical Data

Blockchain contributes to the development of decentralized applications and data-sharing systems. Medical management of data has historically been a computerized system that operates autonomously. The disadvantage of this method of leadership is that it is challenging to establish the true exchange of information due to the overly centralized power of data administration. Shared text features are introduced by blockchain technology. Because several organizations together preserve and oversee the file contents entered in blockchain technology, openness and honesty of data details are guaranteed by the shared oversight by various divisions, which also establishes the transparency and accountability of rules controlling blockchain technology deals. This will essentially address the issues associated with conventional healthcare data administration, such as inadequate work productivity and an overly disorganized atmosphere at work.

Blockchain can also be used to create a reliable transfer mechanism. The simplest and most fundamental procedures of adding, removing, altering, and inquiring constitute the entirety of healthcare records data administration. Nevertheless, blockchain eliminates the two fundamental steps in the management of preserved knowledge deletion and modification and streamlines the handling of preserved data. The technical architecture of blockchain ensures the safety and immutability of data. Each data block in the blockchain further keeps track of the hash algorithm and genesis time of the prior block. This time-marked network architecture increases the rate at which health data is used and makes standard inspection, monitoring, and accountability easier.

Lastly, blockchain can strengthen assignment norms and data exchange. The automation of exchanging historical data can be optimized through the utilization of block link technology and smart agreements. The intelligent agreement is irreversible once it is put into action and is not affected by outside processes. This functionality can be used by hospitals to consolidate stake allocation policies. Smart agreements have the potential to significantly increase the value of healthcare data by encouraging involvement from stakeholders in sharing data, increasing effectiveness and the speed of data trade, and changing member activity. The integrity of healthcare data is guaranteed and the hidden container operation associated with the conventional sharing of data is restrained in this mandatory exchange of data.

### Information Administration in Healthcare Utilizing Blockchain

One of the greatest current advances in computer science is the use of blockchain technology, which enables network members to keep track of activities and quickly exchange them with other users of the blockchain. Four basic groups were put forth in this field to address privacy and security concerns: First, methods for encryption of data and decoding. Second, a fresh approach to electronic identities. The secure transmission of information technique comes in third. The blockchain-based key-generating process comes in at number four.

CouchDB mechanism records every update, much like a blockchain. CouchDb is also called a document-oriented database. It stores and retrieves data as a key value pair, but the value part is stored as a document. The document is stored in (JavaScript Object Notation) JSON formats. ### Zero Knowledge Proof (ZKF)

In the description, the blockchain has a high degree of transparency. All of the data entered into the record is accessible to any node in the Bitcoin system, and the zero-knowledge-proof technique is incorporated into the blockchain to protect the anonymity of individuals and businesses.

## INTERPLANETARY FILE SYSTEM (IPFS)

A network-wide exchange mechanism called IPFS was created to enable dispersed, durable transfer of files and archiving. Like the web, the interplanetary file system (IPFS) is a decentralized peer-to-peer data transmission network. The primary distinction is that, whereas geographically based routing is used on the web, document-based indexing was utilized by IPFS. Since the files cannot be manipulated, data using this document-based referencing is immutable. A novel file is formed when an item is modified. In this peer-to-peer system, files are hashed to establish trust. This trust can be verified by rebuilding the hash algorithm or Merkletree from the unverified material. Massive documents can be chopped using IPFS, which is capable of downloading multiple parts simultaneously as needed. Distributed, global network architecture: By storing substantial volumes of multimedia information in IPFS, this type of network layout can address memory restrictions in the blockchain. Retention of encoded records: IPFS tacks on a hash function to the secret data that is exclusive of electronic data. This code ensures that the associated document cannot be altered. The hash and the file match exactly.

Each document that is uploaded to an IPFS cluster is assigned a distinct hash value that is determined by the information inside the item. When an archive is asked to be obtained, IPFS (Fig. [**9**](#9789815305210-C4.html#f9)) uses its hash table to identify its location and then retrieves its contents. Blockchain and IPFS together may be a useful way to address the blockchain capacity dilemma.

![](Images/9789815305210-C4-F9.jpg)**Fig. (9))**  
Storing medical records in decentralized storage through Hyperledger Fabric. 

Both patients and physicians gain entry to healthcare information by participating in the solution's permission process. Intelligent agreements can be established or updated by administrator clients, who are also in charge of monitoring the blockchain.

## CHALLENGES AND LIMITATIONS

In the healthcare industry, blockchain technology's potential to enhance the handling of data, confidentiality, and privacy \[[23](#9789815305210-C4.html#r23)\] has garnered a lot of interest. Still, there are several barriers to its adoption.

• **Threats to the Safety and Confidentiality of Data**: Broadband capacity and processing power.

• **Exposed Information:** Complicated pharmaceutical tracking scenario.

• **Continuous Surveillance of the Safeguards for Health Information:** Lag in time while checking the blocks.

• **Data Manipulation and Entry Restriction:** Insufficient time to process.

• **Information Protection:** Difficult to identify gadget signs.

• **Clinical Information is Handled and Tracked:** Insufficient connectivity between the gadgets and the data center.

Since HLF is a proprietary blockchain technological advance and no production-grade chain codes were available as open source, we were unwilling to gather data with varying difficulties in performance according to intricacy. Instead, we had to model the amount of code by employing different computations. Low RAM (Random Access Memory) was one of the equipment traits that posed a major obstacle.

## FUTURE OPPORTUNITIES

The world of mankind will be greatly influenced by blockchain technology, and blockchain healthcare has yet to fully realize its promise. Blockchain healthcare will shake up humankind's current order, recognition by law, and financial structures in addition to revolutionizing technology and transforming entire sectors. The following development trends are anticipated to emerge as blockchain technology continues to mature.

### Intelligent Computation

Blockchain innovation can be used in intelligent systems to offer a shared environment and decentralized trade for different intelligence elements, such as information, computations, and computing capacity. This could lead to the development of artificial intelligence and innovative design in a whole new direction. Blockchain will increase openness, simplicity, and decision-reliability in the healthcare sector. Since all blockchain data is accessible to everyone, artificial intelligence will be the cornerstone for confidentiality security and fighting counterfeiting.

### Improve IoT Data Safety and Openness

Blockchain innovation in the healthcare industry has the potential to improve IoT data safety and openness while also promoting future IoT flexibility, effectiveness, and uniformity. Blockchain-enabled Internet of Things (IoT) devices used in healthcare will allow patients to manage who gets access to the data they gather, enhancing the gadget's protection against attackers and giving an accurate track of who has looked at the information. Supply chain transactions may be carried out *via* blockchain-based methods backed by intelligent agreements, depending on the specific conditions monitored by connected devices.

### Privacy in Healthcare

Encrypted medical records that are only accessible by certain individuals can be stored on blockchain. Healthcare involves additional criteria, like data exchange and connectivity, for safeguarding patient data. Interchange is the method of exchanging information with different sources. With the involvement of insurance companies, authorities, medical professionals, and patients, distributed ledgers offer safe and private healthcare administration. Blockchain can be used for a variety of tasks in the medical field. Blockchain technology helps doctors find genetic codes by controlling the pharmaceutical manufacturing process, enabling secure transmission of medical data from patients, and simplifying the exchange of information about patients. Keeping patient health information safe from hackers and ensuring confidentiality through verified access is one of the biggest problems the healthcare sector is now facing. Healthcare growth is built on blockchain security; in the future, blockchain security will primarily develop through technological application, application deepening, and supervisory systems.

## REAL-WORLD EXAMPLES OF SUCCESSFUL BLOCKCHAIN IMPLEMENTATIONS IN HEALTHCARE

The potential of blockchain technology to revolutionize the healthcare sector through increased data security, more openness, and streamlined procedures is demonstrated by several successful blockchain applications in the field.

I. MedRec was developed by MIT Media Lab, a blockchain-based electronic medical record system designed to manage authentication, confidentiality, and data sharing. This system allows patients to control their medical records and provides a transparent audit trail, ensuring data integrity and reducing administrative efforts \[[24](#9789815305210-C4.html#r24)\].

II. Patientory is a blockchain-based solution that focuses on improving data interoperability and patient care management. By storing health information on a blockchain, Patientory enables secure and seamless data sharing among healthcare providers, which can lead to more coordinated and effective patient care \[[25](#9789815305210-C4.html#r25)\].

III. Pharmaceutical Supply Chain: Blockchain technology is being utilized to secure the pharmaceutical supply chain. Companies like chronicled have implemented blockchain to track pharmaceuticals from production to the end consumer, ensuring authenticity and reducing the risk of counterfeit drugs entering the supply chain. This system increases transparency and trust among stakeholders \[[26](#9789815305210-C4.html#r26)\].

IV. Estonian Health Record System employs blockchain to secure patient health records. The system ensures data integrity and provides an immutable audit trail of all access and modifications to health records, significantly enhancing data security and patient trust \[[27](#9789815305210-C4.html#r27)\].

V. Patientdatachain platform uses blockchain to create a decentralized database for medical records, enabling patients to grant access to their records to doctors, hospitals, and labs securely. Patientdatachain ensures that data is accurate, up-to- date, and accessible, thus improving patient care and operational efficiency in healthcare settings \[[28](#9789815305210-C4.html#r28)\].

The above examples demonstrate that blockchain can address significant challenges in healthcare, such as data security, interoperability, and supply chain integrity, leading to more efficient and reliable healthcare systems. All the acronyms used in this manuscript are listed in Table **4**.

## CONCLUSION

Blockchain is decentralized, open, and immutable, and it has the potential to revolutionize healthcare by facilitating secure storage of information, connectivity, and focus on patient treatment. There are many possibilities for this technology, such as performing clinical research and healthcare information, preventing deception, and enhancing the oversight of supply chains (Drugs). The application of blockchain technology to health management of data intends to lower expenses, streamline activities, enhance the health of patients, and do away with middlemen. To advance information systems in hospitals reform and enhance the safety of healthcare information exchange and preservation, Hyperledger technology is being applied in the healthcare sector. Hyperledger is the most practical and user-friendly convey to be utilized when carrying out a corporate cross-industry blockchain solution because it provides variable management of the parameters mentioned earlier within a network on an element level, allowing safety to be implemented in various manners within a single network. A flexible design to build industrial blockchain apps is offered by Hyperledger Fabric. Because Hyperledger Fabric has made specific parts, including consensus processes and membership offerings, the structure is now easily expandable and preserved, rendering it perfect for a variety of uses in industry scenarios. Although this is merely a transitory phase, we ought to worry about resolving the information capacity issue from the blockchain in the future. IPFS and blockchain can help reduce the archive strain of blockchain.
